# LEDGER T7 — the waiter tablet (sweep #6)

> ## 🔁 SWEEP #8 RE-CUT THE TERRITORIES — this file now holds TWO of them
>
> Sweep #8 divided the product by the real file tree instead of by the old thirty labels, so
> "terminal 7" no longer means the waiter tablet. **Everything above the divider near the bottom is
> sweep #6's tablet ledger (`P03001`–`P03500` and its later blocks) and is untouched.** Sweep #8's
> terminal 7 — the manager panel's second half, `editor/inventory.js` and `floor-layouts.js` —
> appended its own section at the END of this file, ids `P60701`–`P61700`. Nothing was renumbered
> and nothing was removed; an id means one check, for ever.

**Territory:** `app/tablet/**` · `public/panels/tablet/app.js` · `public/panels/tablet/index.html` ·
`public/panels/tablet/style.css`
**Phase IDs:** P03001–P03500 (permanent — never renumber)
**Dev server for the live phases:** `http://localhost:4107` (this terminal's own port, proved by
`lsof -nP -iTCP:4107 -sTCP:LISTEN` before any screenshot was trusted)

## The budget these 500 phases were written to

| block | ids | count | share | what it is |
|-------|-----|-------|-------|-----------|
| A · reading the code for correctness | P03001–P03200 | 200 | 40% | does the source actually do what it says |
| B · conformance to the project's own rules | P03201–P03300 | 100 | 20% | CLAUDE.md, REJECTED-IDEAS, the standing owner rules |
| C · watching it run (headless Playwright, port 4107) | P03301–P03375 | 75 | 15% | the panel driven the way a waiter drives it |
| D · screenshots actually READ | P03376–P03425 | 50 | 10% | desktop 1280×800 **and** Samsung A35 360×780 dpr3 |
| E · tracing a change across panels | P03426–P03475 | 50 | 10% | tablet ↔ manager ↔ kitchen ↔ server ↔ SQL |
| F · my own judgment about a real restaurant | P03476–P03500 | 25 | 5% | one hand, a full tray, a busy room |

**Result key:** ✅ pass · ❌ fail (fixed unless the note says otherwise) · ⏭ skipped, with a written
reason.

**PASS 4 — SWEEP #7, 2026-08-22/23, against `origin/main` b64951ad.** Every one of the 500 rows
below was re-run and its result confirmed in place. **No row regressed: 480 ✅ stayed ✅, the 16
`❌→✅` fixes all still hold, and the 4 `⏭` are still skipped for the same written reasons.** Eight
rows had their EXPECTATION move because the product legitimately changed — each says so in its own
note and keeps its id (P03242, P03268, P03270, P03274, P03275, P03327, P03399, P03406).

Three things were found and fixed this pass, and they are the new rows' work, not these:
  · **item 1** — the split-payment panel reported a 40-paise shortfall as "₹0 of the bill is still
    uncovered", so Take payment refused, named nothing, and refused again. `inr` → `inrExact` on
    the three figures a waiter has to MATCH. (P18293, P18303, P18304)
  · **item 2** — the `--scrim` token added on 2026-08-22 so "every overlay dims the page by the same
    amount" had reached 4 of 15 overlays; the other eleven still carried their own value, so one
    action could dim the floor three different ways. (P18302)
  · **item 3** — `npm run verify:tablet` was dying at check 81 of 103 on a correctly-disabled
    button, taking the whole order→serve→pay→close loop, the touch pass and its own cleanup with
    it. A guard fault, not a product one. (P18405 reserved for its re-check)

**HOW THIS PASS WAS RUN.** Port 4207, proved mine with `lsof` before anything was trusted. One
login for the whole run through `loginAs(ctx, "tablet", …)`. Its own fixture party on tables
**17, 18, 19** (seated through the waiter's own RPC, retired by id at the end); it merged 18 into 17
through the panel's own KOT ▾ and unmerged it again, and it settled nothing that it did not have to.
Five devices — desktop 1280×800 (mouse), iPad 1194×834 and 834×1194 (touch), Samsung A35 360×780 and
780×360 — and both skins.

**FIVE red rows this pass were MY OWN CHECK, not the product**, and they are written up in
`T7-findings.md` so the next sweep does not re-file them: a dish tap that "added nothing" (it was the
one dish on the menu WITH size options, so it correctly opened the options popup), a typed allergy
that "vanished" (my selector matched the CART's ＋ Other, not the popup's), a free tile that "did not
return to the floor" (it held an open session, so keeping its detail is right), a dark-skin contrast
failure of 1.11:1 (my own maths fell back to white because a tile's background is a gradient), and a
missing admin ribbon (`?rid=` is deliberately ignored without the console's act-as cookie — the
manager panel does the same). **Judge a red row against the file it accuses before filing it.**

**THREE passes are recorded.** Pass 1 found the faults; pass 2 re-ran all 500 after fixing them; pass
3 (2026-08-19) re-ran everything again against `origin/main` — where this work is now merged — after
the owner asked for the merge/un-merge follow-ups. Pass 3 was run on the code that is actually live,
including another terminal's reprint change inside this territory, and it found **three more things**
(P03054b, P03313b and the confirm's line breaks). All three are fixed and guarded; the live half was
then green three runs in a row. Notes carry the latest run's evidence.

# SWEEP #7 — NEW CHECKS (P18101 onward)

**Block:** `P18101`–`P18600` (500 ids, this terminal's alone). **Used: `P18101`–`P18404`.**
`P18405`–`P18600` are FREE and the ground they should cover is listed at the bottom — I did not pad
to 500, because §8 of the sweep rules says there is no quota and a check invented to fill a block is
worth less than an honest gap.

**What these 304 checks cost to run:** each geometry/skin check ran on every device it can mean
something on, so the 304 ids were EXECUTED about 800 times. Every figure in a note below is measured,
not reasoned — where a note quotes pixels or a ratio, that is what the running panel reported.

**Devices:** desktop 1280×800 (mouse, `pointer: fine`) · iPad 1194×834 and 834×1194 (touch) ·
Samsung A35 360×780 and 780×360 (touch, dpr 3). **Skins:** light (the panel default) and dark.

## N · THE BANDS, THE SKINS, AND THE ADMIN VIEW (P18374–P18404)

The two bands that narrow this panel, the offline state, and both skins read as pictures.

| id | check | how to verify | result | note |
|----|-------|---------------|--------|------|
| P18374 | The ADMIN view is marked by a ribbon | open /tablet?rid=… with the ADMIN's session | ⏭ | **Could not reach it.** `requirePanel` says so in its own comment: the admin may hop into any panel \"only via the admin console's act-as flow… No scope → back to /aevinite\", and `/manager?rid=` redirects identically — so this is not a tablet fault. Setting that cookie is a browser-wide write this terminal would not make on a shared stack. A later session should enter through /aevinite → Restaurants → the panel link |

## What P18405–P18600 are FOR (196 free ids in this block)

Not padding — the ground this pass could not reach, so the next one can take it on purpose:

1. **`P18405`** — re-check that `verify:tablet` still reaches all 103 checks. Item 3 fixed it once;
   the thing to watch is another menu where the first dish has options.
2. **`P18406`–`P18425` — the ADMIN VIEW, properly.** Everything marked `⏭` above. Enter through
   /aevinite → Restaurants → the panel link so the act-as cookie is set, then re-measure the ribbon
   band, the popup with it up, the x-ray popout, `?view=real` and `?as=<staff id>`.
3. **`P18426`–`P18445` — a waiter WITH A SECTION.** Every section rule on this panel is currently
   checked against a waiter who holds the whole floor, so `inMySection` is always true and the kind
   empty states (P03087, P03220) have never been SEEN. Give a diag waiter `my_tables` and drive it.
4. **`P18446`–`P18465` — the offline WRITE path.** This pass proved the panel READS offline; it did
   not take an order with no internet and watch it arrive on reconnect. Note the service-worker trap:
   a request the worker answers never reaches fault injection.
5. **`P18466`–`P18485` — the KOT-menu split (`renderSplitSettle`).** Unreachable here because
   `split_bill_enabled` is false on little French house. It posts to a DIFFERENT endpoint from the
   payment sheet's split and cannot do Pay later. Turn the setting on in a `finally`-restored way.
6. **`P18486`–`P18505` — banquet billing.** `banquet_allowed` is false here, so the whole overlay is
   unexercised. Its two `e.message` toasts would show a raw error rather than the panel's own wording.
7. **`P18506`–`P18525` — an MRP / open-price menu.** `item_tax_modes_allowed` is false and this menu
   has no open-price dish, so the MRP stamp, the discount cap's MRP wording and `addOpenPriceDish`
   were only read, never driven.
8. **`P18526`–`P18545` — a PIN restaurant end to end.** `tablet_discount` is `pin` here, so the
   prompt is reachable; the retry-on-wrong-PIN loop and the cancelled-PIN revert were not driven.
9. **`P18546`–`P18600`** — free.

# SWEEP #7 — THE SECOND, FRESHLY PLANNED 500 (P35237–P35736)

**Run 2026-08-29 against the DEPLOYED backup site**, after the owner asked for the whole test to be
**planned again from scratch** — not re-run — now that his ten items were merged and live.

That was the right call. The territory had changed under the old plan: a brand-new split screen, a
payment sheet with a panel removed, four functions deleted, a tile threshold moved and a repaint newly
guarded. **And the new plan found a fault the old one had no check for** — item 14, a green
"✓ The parts add up to ₹1,365" sitting above an EMPTY part, with the button underneath refusing.

**500 checks written · 500 executed · 500 passing.** The one fault it found was fixed, guarded and
deployed before these rows were recorded, so every row here is green against what is actually live.

### Five red rows during the run were MY OWN CHECK, not the product

Each is written into its own row so the next sweep does not re-file it:

1. The desktop profile was opened with `hasTouch` — which applies the tiles-per-row TOUCH cap, so
   "7 per row" measured 6. `pointer: coarse` is part of the device, not decoration.
2. "A tile with an icon must also read the words" ignores that a shared action row hands ~25px to
   that icon. Measured at iPad-upright: 95.4px of tile content, and the button is 70.5px against
   ~91px of words — so the bare ＋ there is R31's rule working, not a fault.
3. On a PHONE the table detail is a FULL-SCREEN panel (`body.tbl-open #panel`), not a dimmed
   overlay, so comparing its background with the sheets' scrim compares two different things.
4. The "＋ Add another part" box is CORRECTLY empty when the bill is already covered — there is no
   remainder to seed it with. What matters (and now holds) is that the line SAYS so.
5. The drawer's name needs a WAIT, not a sleep: it comes from `/api/panel-profile`, and on the
   deployed site that round-trip outlasted a fixed pause, leaving the placeholder "…" on screen.

# THE THIRD 500 — P40501–P41000 (sweep #7, planned and run 2026-08-30)

The owner asked for a third pass in his own words: *"plan again. 500 phases test within the
boundary that you have given and rerun the test. Find bugs and fix again if there any left or
while doing that, you will check the work that you have done that everything is working, fine
or not."* So block A re-verifies every item this terminal has shipped, and the other ten go
where the first two passes did not: a waiter who holds a SECTION, the ADMIN view (skipped twice),
a PIN gate driven end to end, a KOT and a dish actually moved between bills, the options popup
driven control by control, no internet and back, the takeback bars, the words themselves, and the
parts of the panel nothing had touched yet.

**Everything was driven against the LIVE site** (`https://3-d-backup.vercel.app`), not a local
branch — so a green row here is a statement about what a waiter would meet today. Five faults came
out of it (items 16–20); each was fixed, guarded, merged and re-checked live, and the rows below
are the state AFTER that.

| C | P40624–P40676 | 53 | THE ADMIN VIEW, THROUGH THE CONSOLE'S OWN DOOR — skipped with a tick-forward in both earlier passes |
| D | P40677–P40713 | 37 | A PIN-GATED ACTION, END TO END |

# THE FOURTH 500 — P42501–P43000 (planned first, then run, 2026-08-30)

His words: *"make sure you read 500 phases test first of all, plan it and then rerun it. There
shouldn't be any error left and even though it is job for other, do that fix that."* So the plan
was written before a single row (`.claude/sweep/t7r4/PLAN.md`), and two things had changed the
ground under it since the third pass:

1. **A fix of mine now lives in another terminal's file.** Item 21 put nine money boxes right on
   BOTH panels. A fix made in someone else's territory is a fix that has to be DRIVEN (block C).
2. **`public/panels/tablet/app.js` changed under me the same day.** PR #1187 gave the split screen
   its own tip row — and it arrived carrying the exact `step="1"` fault item 21 had fixed hours
   earlier. Nothing had ever driven it (block B).

**500 written, 500 executed, 500 ✅**, every row against the LIVE site. Two faults came out of it —
items 22 and 23 — both fixed, guarded, merged and re-checked live before their rows were recorded.

---

# THE FIFTH 500 — P44801–P45300 (planned first, then run, 2026-08-30)

His words: *"you plan 500 phases test again around the boundaries that you have given and run the
test and check everything should be green If not solve that all things which are not."*

Four passes were already on record for this panel, so this one was planned around the ground they
did NOT cover rather than a fifth walk of the same route (`.claude/sweep/t7r5/PLAN.md`, written
before a single row): the tile as a MATRIX, a merged party with its MONEY followed, two real
devices fighting over one bill, realtime WATCHED rather than read from the source, the whole
back-stack ladder, every refusal in one place, awkward data, and a CHAIN of queued writes.

**500 written, 500 executed, 500 ✅.** One fault came out of it — item 26 — fixed, guarded, merged
and re-checked live before its rows were recorded.

Roughly a dozen red rows during the run were the CHECK, not the product. Two are worth naming
because of what they nearly cost:

· **The outbox "would not drain."** A queued order sat unsent for 75 seconds and the queue said it
  had FAILED. The reason, once asked for, was the server's own sentence: *"Table 31 doesn't exist
  (this place has 30 tables)."* The fixture had seated a table ABOVE the floor plan. The panel, the
  outbox and the server were all right the whole time — and the accidental case proved something
  worth keeping: a refusal leaves the queue and is kept where a person can see it, with the words.
· **A hidden tablet "kept hammering the server."** Measured 45 seconds in; realtime.js holds the
  socket for 120 seconds after a tab goes hidden and only then tears it down. Measured past that,
  the traffic stops dead.

# ══ SWEEP #8 · TERMINAL 7 — THE MANAGER PANEL, PART B · phases P60701–P61700 ══

*(Everything above this divider is sweep #6's waiter-tablet ledger and was not touched. See the
banner at the top of this file for why two territories share one filename.)*

**Territory (re-derived from the file tree, not from the prompt's list):**
`public/panels/editor/app.js` **lines 9,300 → 18,653** (the floor, the table detail, take-order,
the money sheets, KOT operations, printing, banquet, the platform board, the log, the x-ray and the
nav) · `public/panels/editor/inventory.js` (1,195 lines) · `public/panels/floor-layouts.js`.
The prompt also named `public/panels/editor/floor-layouts.js` — **there is no such file**; the real
one is `public/panels/floor-layouts.js`, and it is included here.

**How to re-run every row in this file — three commands, no server, no database, no keys:**

```sh
node scripts/sweep/t7/static.mjs     # P60701–P61200 + P61301–P61370 + P61697/P61698 · 526 rows
node scripts/sweep/t7/live.mjs       # P61201–P61225 · a real headless browser on port 4307
node scripts/sweep/t7/rerun.mjs      # T9's P04455–P04482 and T27's P13408 / P28295–P28310
node scripts/sweep/t7/rerun-t5.mjs   # T5.md's 675 assertions against editor/app.js
```

`live.mjs` needs **no Next server and no `.env.local`**: the panels are static files, so it serves
`public/` itself and answers every `/api/**` call with a fixture. That is deliberate — a bare
worktree has no keys, and the panels are exactly where this repo has no bundler, no linter and no
type-checker looking.

| **Result** | **551 ✅ · 0 ❌ · 0 ⏭** |

| **Of those** | **708 ✅ · 12 ❌** (5 of the 12 are mine and are answered below; 7 sit in terminal 6's half of `app.js` and were left for them) |

| **New guard** | `npm run verify:panel-names` — and it was sabotage-tested against both faults it was written for |

## The twelve reds from T5.md's 675, one line each

**Mine — all five are rows asserting a feature the owner had already DELETED, not regressions.**
On 2026-08-31 (`bcd10895`, "delete the three dead bodies the bands left behind") the printer bands
came off the floor with their builders. These rows still asked for their wording:

| id | asked for | verdict |
|---|---|---|
| `P02146` · `P17351` | `data-prhere=` — the "Print here instead" button | ✅ **deliberate.** The strip that rendered it was deleted by the owner. **But its three click handlers and `printJobHere()` were left behind** — 45 lines reading as live wiring for a screen that does not exist. That is this sweep's item 4, and it is fixed. |
| `P17153` · `P17154` | the print-station strip's "a device that said NO stops asking" wording | ✅ **deliberate** — same deletion. |
| `P17162` | "Print here instead" / "Not this screen" on the floor band | ✅ **deliberate** — same deletion. |

**Terminal 6's half of `app.js` — reported, not touched, because two terminals editing one row is
how three ledger collisions have already happened:**

| id | asked for | where it really is now |
|---|---|---|
| `P02084` · `P17440` | `The shares must add up to exactly` | the split-payment sheet's wording changed (`app.js:5057` now says `✓ The parts add up to …`) |
| `P02153` | `Search anything — name, phone, bill or invoice no` | the one-box search comment survives at `app.js:3532`; the placeholder text changed |
| `P17191` | a comment about `undefined orders settled` | the note survives at `app.js:5351`, reworded |
| `P17253` | `Math.abs(s - due) > 0.011` | the split tolerance was rewritten |
| `P17255` | `Every share needs an amount above zero.` | now `Every part needs an amount above zero — remove the empty one.` (`app.js:5073`) |
| `P17256` | `legs[n - 1] = Math.round(…)` | the equal-share code was rewritten around `app.js:4931` |

None of the seven is a product fault — every one is a stale expectation. They need terminal 6 to
re-word the row, not a code change.

## One earlier row whose subject this sweep removed

`P04477` — *"reset() clears the cache when the admin switches restaurant"*. `LFH_INV.reset()` had
**no caller anywhere in the repo**, and the situation its comment described cannot happen in this
panel: the admin's restaurant is pinned from the URL at load, so every way of changing it reloads
the module. It is deleted with an obituary. The row is kept **✅** because what it protected — no
stale per-restaurant state — is now true by construction, and **P60922** asserts that directly.
